			log = "GALMARKET_ASSET: guard detail [This.GetRealName] ind=[This.gsm_ind] variant=[This.gsm_dbg_v] on [gsm_t_site.GetName] owner [gsm_t_uown.GetName] sr_living_metal: need=[This.gsm_t_need_sr_living_metal] unit_upkeep=[This.gsm_dbg_uu_sr_living_metal] unit_output=[This.gsm_dbg_uo_sr_living_metal] eff=[This.gsm_dbg_cue] auto=[This.gsm_t_ga] blended=[This.gsm_t_gbe] inc=[This.gsm_dbg_ginc] rev=[This.gsm_dbg_grv] exp=[This.gsm_dbg_gex] stock=[This.gsm_dbg_gst] stock_term=[This.gsm_dbg_gstk] pend_m=[This.gsm_dbg_gpu_m] pend_p=[This.gsm_dbg_gpu_p] head=[This.gsm_dbg_ghead] cap=[This.gsm_dbg_gcap] ucap=[This.gsm_t_ucap]"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = gsm_t_need_sr_zro value > 0 } }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_ginc value = event_target:gsm_t_uown.gsm_inc_sr_zro }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_gstk value = event_target:gsm_t_uown.gsm_t_stk_sr_zro }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_gpu_m value = event_target:gsm_t_uown.gsm_pu_sr_zro_m }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_gpu_p value = event_target:gsm_t_uown.gsm_pu_sr_zro_p }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_ghead value = event_target:gsm_t_uown.gsm_t_head_sr_zro }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_grv value = event_target:gsm_t_uown.gsm_dbg_rv_sr_zro }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_gex value = event_target:gsm_t_uown.gsm_dbg_ex_sr_zro }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_gst value = event_target:gsm_t_uown.gsm_dbg_st_sr_zro }
			set_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_gcap value = gsm_dbg_ghead }
			gsm_clamp_min = { VAR = gsm_dbg_gcap MIN = 0 }
			divide_variable = { which = gsm_dbg_gcap value = gsm_t_need_sr_zro }
			gsm_clamp_max = { VAR = gsm_dbg_gcap MAX = @gsm_ucap_none }
			gsm_floor = { VAR = gsm_dbg_gcap }
			log = "GALMARKET_ASSET: guard detail [This.GetRealName] ind=[This.gsm_ind] variant=[This.gsm_dbg_v] on [gsm_t_site.GetName] owner [gsm_t_uown.GetName] sr_zro: need=[This.gsm_t_need_sr_zro] unit_upkeep=[This.gsm_dbg_uu_sr_zro] unit_output=[This.gsm_dbg_uo_sr_zro] eff=[This.gsm_dbg_cue] auto=[This.gsm_t_ga] blended=[This.gsm_t_gbe] inc=[This.gsm_dbg_ginc] rev=[This.gsm_dbg_grv] exp=[This.gsm_dbg_gex] stock=[This.gsm_dbg_gst] stock_term=[This.gsm_dbg_gstk] pend_m=[This.gsm_dbg_gpu_m] pend_p=[This.gsm_dbg_gpu_p] head=[This.gsm_dbg_ghead] cap=[This.gsm_dbg_gcap] ucap=[This.gsm_t_ucap]"
		}
	}
}
